6:40:566 hours, 40 minutes, 56 seconds Is a small, densely populated territory along the eastern Mediterranean coast bordered by Israel and Egypt. It is home
6:41:036 hours, 41 minutes, 3 seconds to over 2.2 2 million people, making it one of the most densely populated areas in the world. The area has been continuously inhabited for millennia,
6:41:126 hours, 41 minutes, 12 seconds influenced by Egyptian, Roman, Byzantine, and Islamic civilizations,
6:41:176 hours, 41 minutes, 17 seconds making it an incredibly historically and culturally rich region, and a crossroads of civilizations for thousands of years.
6:41:256 hours, 41 minutes, 25 seconds Roughly 70% of the population of Gaza are refugees or descendants of families displaced during earlier conflicts.
6:41:326 hours, 41 minutes, 32 seconds Despite decades of economic and political challenges, the people of Gaza built a thriving society.
6:41:386 hours, 41 minutes, 38 seconds Gaza city became densely urbanized with modern apartment blocks, shopping centers, and bustling streets. The seafront was a hub of daily life with
6:41:466 hours, 41 minutes, 46 seconds cafes, restaurants, and markets. Gaza had 80 public libraries and 76 registered cultural centers such as the
6:41:546 hours, 41 minutes, 54 seconds Rashad Al-Sha Cultural Center, Shabbabek for contemporary art, and the Alcarara Museum. Around 220,000 people
6:42:026 hours, 42 minutes, 2 seconds participated in cultural activities. The Edward said National Conservatory of Music trained young musicians. Street
6:42:106 hours, 42 minutes, 10 seconds murals and graffiti turned the walls of Gaza into canvases expressing both struggle and pride. Parks, playgrounds,
6:42:176 hours, 42 minutes, 17 seconds and cafes were filled with families.
6:42:196 hours, 42 minutes, 19 seconds Weddings were huge social events celebrated with traditional music,
6:42:236 hours, 42 minutes, 23 seconds dabke, and feasts. Soccer was wildly popular with leagues, youth training programs, and even women
6:42:306 hours, 42 minutes, 30 seconds women's teams forming. Gaza developed strong educational systems which included a total of 28 higher education institutions such as the Islamic University of Gaza, Alazar and Al Axa.
6:42:426 hours, 42 minutes, 42 seconds The region boasted one of the highest literacy rates in the world, reaching an astounding 95%. Its agriculture cultivated olives,
6:42:506 hours, 42 minutes, 50 seconds strawberries, citrus, and flowers. Small ports supported fishermen who brought in daily catches from the Mediterranean.
6:42:576 hours, 42 minutes, 57 seconds Traditional souks, markets, sold spices,
6:43:006 hours, 43 minutesclothing, and handmade crafts, mixing old traditions with modern goods. Thanks to its people's resilience and ingenuity, Gaza's infrastructure allowed daily life to function, and culture,
6:43:116 hours, 43 minutes, 11 seconds education, and industry flourished even under incredible hardships.

6:47:056 hours, 47 minutes, 5 seconds Since October 7th, 2023, Gaza has faced one of the worst humanitarian crises in modern history. Over 65,000 people have
6:47:126 hours, 47 minutes, 12 seconds been killed and approximately 90% of Gaza's population has been displaced.
6:47:176 hours, 47 minutes, 17 seconds Homes, schools, hospitals, markets, and cultural centers have been destroyed or severely damaged.
6:47:246 hours, 47 minutes, 24 seconds Power infrastructure has largely collapsed, leaving more than 2 million people without reliable electricity.
6:47:306 hours, 47 minutes, 30 seconds About 98% of crop land is reported damaged or inaccessible in Gaza. In August 2025, famine was confirmed in
6:47:386 hours, 47 minutes, 38 seconds Gaza. According to the integrated food security phase classification, half a million people in Gaza are facing catastrophic hunger and 96% of the population faces acute food insecurity.
6:47:506 hours, 47 minutes, 50 seconds Over 80% of healthc care facilities are damaged or nonfunctional, leaving routine vaccinations, emergency
6:47:586 hours, 47 minutes, 58 seconds surgeries, and treatment for chronic conditions nearly impossible. In many hospitals, occupancy is far above
6:48:056 hours, 48 minutes, 5 seconds capacity, some at 150 to 200%, with certain facilities reporting full saturation at 300% capacity.
6:48:136 hours, 48 minutes, 13 seconds Twothirds of Gaza's water and sanitation infrastructure is non-functional,
6:48:176 hours, 48 minutes, 17 seconds increasing the risk of disease outbreaks and leaving over 1.5 million without access to safe drinking water. More than
6:48:246 hours, 48 minutes, 24 seconds 97% of the water pumped from the coastal aquifer does not meet the water quality standards of the World Health Organization. Fuel shortages are
6:48:326 hours, 48 minutes, 32 seconds threatening the operation of critical infrastructure. Dr. Dr. Marwan Al Hams,
6:48:376 hours, 48 minutes, 37 seconds director of Gaza Hospitals, stated that nearly 50% of kidney failure patients have died since October 2023 due to low
6:48:456 hours, 48 minutes, 45 seconds reserves. The trauma, fear, and loss affect every aspect of daily life.
6:48:526 hours, 48 minutes, 52 seconds Families live under constant fear, and tens of thousands are displaced into overcrowded shelters with an average of 8 to 10 people per small room.
6:49:016 hours, 49 minutes, 1 secondThe impact on children is particularly devastating. The majority of the population in Gaza is under 18. Infants and toddlers face life-threatening
6:49:096 hours, 49 minutes, 9 seconds conditions, lacking sufficient milk or formula, and many live separated from caregivers in overcrowded shelters.
6:49:166 hours, 49 minutes, 16 seconds Children grow up surrounded by destruction. More than 2,500 schools have been damaged or completely
6:49:226 hours, 49 minutes, 22 seconds destroyed, depriving over 645,000 children of education. Safe play is impossible, and daily life is dominated
6:49:316 hours, 49 minutes, 31 seconds by fear and trauma. Many have witnessed the destruction of homes or the loss of family members. Psychological trauma is widespread.
6:49:406 hours, 49 minutes, 40 seconds UNICEF estimates that over 75% of children in Gaza now show signs of severe stress, anxiety, or depression.
6:49:486 hours, 49 minutes, 48 seconds As of June 2025, at least 17,000 children were estimated to be unaccompanied or separated from their families. Over 63,000 children have been
6:49:586 hours, 49 minutes, 58 seconds killed or injured with many facing life-threatening conditions due to malnutrition and lack of medical care.
6:50:046 hours, 50 minutes, 4 seconds Malnutrition rates have surged with over 66,000 children suffering from severe malnutrition.
6:50:106 hours, 50 minutes, 10 seconds Amid this crisis, the Palestine Children's Relief Fund or PCRF has become a lifeline for countless children
6:50:176 hours, 50 minutes, 17 seconds and families in Gaza. Their work spans medical care, nutrition, water, and mental health support, reaching those
6:50:246 hours, 50 minutes, 24 seconds who are most vulnerable. On the medical front, PCRF has restored operating rooms at Al Axa Hospital, enabling life-saving
6:50:326 hours, 50 minutes, 32 seconds surgeries and treatments for children injured in the conflict. For children who require specialized care unavailable locally, PCRF has coordinated transfers
6:50:416 hours, 50 minutes, 41 seconds to hospitals abroad, giving them access to treatments that could mean the difference between life and death.
6:50:476 hours, 50 minutes, 47 seconds Nutrition is another critical area where PCRF has made a tangible impact. They have distributed over 10,000 emergency
6:50:566 hours, 50 minutes, 56 seconds food parcels to displaced families across Gaza. Infants are supported through emergency formula distributions.
6:51:036 hours, 51 minutes, 3 seconds 4,450 cans of infant formula reached 2,200 newborns who otherwise would have gone hungry.
6:51:116 hours, 51 minutes, 11 seconds Orphans, particularly those who have lost one or both parents, have continued to receive targeted support, helping them survive and maintain some stability amid chaos.
6:51:216 hours, 51 minutes, 21 seconds Recognizing the collapse of infrastructure, PCRF has also delivered clean drinking water directly to families who have no other access,
6:51:296 hours, 51 minutes, 29 seconds preventing disease and helping preserve life. Equally important, PCRF provides psychological support for children,
6:51:366 hours, 51 minutes, 36 seconds actively providing individual and group therapy, recreational activities,
6:51:406 hours, 51 minutes, 40 seconds awareness sessions, and family counseling to help children cope, heal, and rebuild their sense of security. Despite the extreme dangers, shortages,
6:51:496 hours, 51 minutes, 49 seconds and logistical challenges, PCRF's local teams remain committed on the ground.
6:51:546 hours, 51 minutes, 54 seconds Every parcel, every surgery, and every intervention reaches children who have lost almost everything. Even a small contribution can make a real difference.
6:52:036 hours, 52 minutes, 3 seconds $10 can provide hygiene essentials for a displaced family. $50 can supply a family with enough food to sustain them
6:52:106 hours, 52 minutes, 10 seconds for several days. $100 can provide a mattress and warm clothing to someone who has lost everything. $500 can help
6:52:176 hours, 52 minutes, 17 seconds fund critical medical aid for ill and injured children. $1,000 or more can support large-scale relief efforts such
6:52:256 hours, 52 minutes, 25 seconds as preparing hot meals for entire shelters.
